- **MRK** · **NEW PHASE 3** · [NCT07951515](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07951515) P2/P3 · NOT_YET_RECRUITING · primary completion 2029-11-19 — A Clinical Trial of MK-8748 Compared to Aflibercept in Participants With Macular Edema After Retinal Vein Occlusion (MK-8748-006) (Retinal Vein Occlusion, Macular Edema)
